## 2026-10-28 V3范围与逐命题证据复用

四个r_SMe_harder_than_*复用同定义气相电子De：H139.4422678/Cl157.3375525/Me152.7838492/OMe173.6950795/SMe198.5277430 kJ/mol，对应SMe减OMe/Cl/Me/H=24.8326635/41.1901905/45.7438938/59.0854752 kJ/mol。r_sulfur_localized_radical复用SMe原生Hirshfeld S=.400951、苄位C=.021899、羟基O=.007218及完整空间自旋场/对照系列；不再分别奖励3个原子人口。r_oxidation_interpretation复用模型范围说明和原生端点，支持“分子层面一致，但不足确证氩气下完整催化因果”，没有H受体/TS/催化速率证据。五个旧绝对De及三个原子spin窗口转为scored=false作者路线证据，未追值改窗；PR原披露不变。

证据缺口：没有实际H受体、TS、速率决定步或完整催化网络验证；不得由De和局域自旋推定速率/转化率；SI SMe水相电子能正号错误与TableS9 H热参考/标准态实施歧义未解决；扩展热力学不是本轮目标；OMe原异盆地De172.5946171与官方盆地173.6950795并存；后者54/51正模有效，前者历史不删除；O自旋.191746对源.191仍有.000746残差。本轮未重新计算、未进行盲测或语义评分验证；以下历史正文逐字保留，不继承旧PASS为V3全部通过。活动标准以scoring_rules.json为准。

# V2 验证计算参考：苄醇自由基阳离子

论文：`paper_d83e607f125440cc`；模式：`autonomous_research`；整理日期：2026-10-15。

本文件位于私有 evaluation，用于维护者复核真实计算，不能作为被评估 agent 的公开输入。只整理已有已执行证据；本轮没有重算、改题、改规则或改容差。

**验收类型：冻结 V2 科学范围内独立严格通过（PASS_STRICT_CURRENT_FROZEN_SCOPE）。** 这不表示作者整篇所有数字、实验现象或题外机理都已重现。

当前验收依据：五个同定义核心源量均落在源0.1 kJ/mol打印精度内，并有电子解释和端点有效性支持。

当前裁定来自 [正式最新裁定](../../../../docs/v2_verification/history/v2_strict_reaudit_20261014/FINAL_VERDICTS.json) 和 [逐篇详细复审](../../../../docs/v2_verification/history/v2_strict_reaudit_20261014/root_results.json)；这些裁定优先于归档中的历史 PASS、candidate 或 HOLD 字样。



## 1. 科学问题、模式范围与数据来源

五种取代基如何改变自由基阳离子的电子分布及失H能量，与光氧化选择性有何关系。

冻结题面中的科学目标：

Investigate what can be established at the molecular level about the different oxidation behavior of the supplied para-substituted benzyl alcohols. Develop and test an explanation for the comparatively poor conversion of the methylsulfanyl alcohol toward its aldehyde under the reported flavin-mediated electrophotochemical conditions. Determine which parts of that explanation your evidence supports, which remain unresolved, and whether the molecular evidence is sufficient to explain the observation.

自主科研模式向 agent 提供科学问题、物种/数据和边界，由其自主选择方法。以下作者路线是维护者验证采用的参考，不要求 agent 照抄该流程，也不是一次新的盲测自主发现记录。

## 2. 作者路线、实际实现与计算依赖

作者B3LYP/6-311+G(d)真实气相优化/频率、H原子参照和Hirshfeld自旋，水相端点单列；OMe采用官方源几何盆地。

1. 核对 H/Cl/Me/OMe/SMe 五种完整对位取代苄醇及自由基阳离子、失 H 产物，守恒计量为 (+1,doublet)→(+1,singlet)+H(0,doublet)。不是去质子，电子 De 不加入 ZPE。

2. 复用十四个已有效端点并核收十六个新增气相/CPCM 端点及 H 热参考，使用 B3LYP/6-311+G(d)；修正早先 OMe 的错误盆地，并完成源几何频率、小力与网格/自旋控制。

3. 从实际相同参照能量计算 De=E(product)+E(H)−E(parent)，五个源几何气相结果为 139.4422678/157.3375525/152.7838492/173.6950795/198.5277430 kJ/mol，恢复源 139.4/157.3/152.8/173.7/198.5。

4. 按完整 Table S8 的原子/基团映射检查 Hirshfeld 自旋，SMe 的 S=0.400951 对源 0.401；OMe O≈0.191748 与预定 SuperFine 控制的残差另列。不能把不同原子或总基团值混作同一个人口。

5. De 与 S 自旋局域支持 SMe 后续失 H 较难的分子解释；未计算实际受 H 体/TS/速率决定步，不把该能量换成转化率，也不把有氧竞争讨论当作惰性标准条件的定量原因。

### kp_molecular_findings — Traceable molecular evidence relevant to the observed oxidation contrast.

冻结要求：Inspect native scientific outputs and reproducible analyses, recompute reported values and confirm method suitability. Results must distinguish relevant behavior across the chemical comparison actually used. Source BDE/spin numbers or copied old scalar values earn no new evidence credit. If ionization/dissociation is reported, require coherent component energies and definitions; if spin/localization is invoked, require the stated partition and actual mapped data. Equally relevant valid observables may earn the same credit without these particular calculations.

### kp_explanatory_discrimination — Evidence that establishes the reach or failure of the proposed molecular explanation.

冻结要求：Judge whether the chosen comparison can challenge the explanation rather than merely restate the SMe observation. Require actual evidence connecting any cause claimed to the oxidation contrast and addressing material confounding for that claim. An electronic descriptor correlation is not a proved rate-controlling step. Supported rejection, competing interpretations or demonstrated insufficiency can earn full credit when evidentially decisive. Do not require two hypotheses, SMe/OMe solvent controls or a designated heldout compound.

### kp_reliability_and_limits — Validity and uncertainty sufficient for the magnitude and interpretation claimed.

冻结要求：Check stationarity/curvature only where minima are claimed, spin consistency where open-shell species are used, and comparable references where phases or states differ. Require a justified treatment or bound of the dominant uncertainty for the actual explanation, backed by calculations/analyses where necessary. Electronic continuum differences cannot be mislabeled solution free energies. Appropriate analytical bounds or evidence-based restricted claims are accepted; no fixed solvent, functional, conformer count or test matrix is required. Source-context notes govern PR fidelity only; AR remains method-independent and is not required to compute De, spin or TableS9. SI p49 reference11 identifies Gaussian 16 Rev. C.01. SI p34 TableS9 separately reports free-energy-based dissociation equilibria at 298.15 K; the methods state a 1-liter solution standard-volume correction. This thermochemical quantity is distinct from the electronic De and spin baseline requested here. The full TableS9 thermochemical matrix is not a mandatory deliverable or a hidden PR fidelity requirement. For an optional equilibrium/free-energy extension, declare thermal, solvation and standard-state conventions and the neutral H-atom reference consistently; the source does not fully specify the H-reference thermal implementation. Transparent justified substitutions are permissible and must not be represented as recovered author facts. Do not add ZPE/thermal terms to values labeled electronic De.

Partial claims earn only their supported coverage; honest execution failure is not fabrication.

## 5. 实际计算时长与资源

已有逐日志核验的时长摘要：**正常结束阶段累计 4.63 小时；失败/状态未确认阶段 1.20 秒；69 份计时日志/114 个计时段；部分覆盖**。

已记录资源：Gaussian 16 C.01；原生设置 4,20 线程/进程；Gaussian %Mem=100GB,12000MB,12GB,40GB。

CPU/内存为原生设置或明确提交请求，不是峰值实测用量；历史配置原样保留。CPU 时间与 elapsed 不相加，并行任务累计不能解释为日历时间。全流程壁钟、排队、未记录阶段与峰值内存均**未知**；未知不记为 0。

## 6. 已知偏差、失败分支和不能外推的结论

De是电子解离能，不是活化势垒或自由能；OMe氧自旋0.191746与源0.191的末位差保留，不证明整条溶液反应机理。

次要OMe O spin源0.191 vs0.191746不是精确末位吻合；不改变当前必需硫定位/De结论。不认证真正H转移速率、未规定的完整催化机制或TableS9热平衡。


原独立审查的 `acceptance_limits`（如与后续状态冲突，以第 1 节最终裁决为准）：

```json
[
  "OMeoxygen printedspin .191 versus .191746 is an unresolved secondarynumericresidual; itisnot calledexactlyreproduced.",
  "SourceSMe-waterpositiveelectronicenergy is explicitly rejectedasphysicalreference; optionalTableS9 H/standard-state conventionsnotfullyrecoverable.",
  "ElectronicDe isnotthermalBDE,activationfreeenergy,rateoryield.",
  "Nooxygen-side-reactionquantitativecausalityunderargon claimed;sourceconditions keptdistinct.",
  "Strict validation isforcurrentboundedscientificproblem anditsauthorbaseline,notblindARorformaljudgeperformance."
]
```


科学参考验证完成不等于语义评审器已校准或运行时隔离已测试；旧 validation_plan 中的工程检查状态未在本次文档整理中冒称完成。

归档依据：[本篇最终严格复审](../../../../docs/v2_verification/papers/paper_d83e607f125440cc/task_archives/autonomous_research/evaluation/verified_computation/strict_verdict.json)。本轮仅移动未通过任务、汇总既有实际计算、补正文档并更新包哈希，没有改变题目或评分规则。

## 2026-10-19 active paper_science_v2 scoring migration

The active private rubric assigns 100 points only to independent intermediate findings and final scientific conclusions, using binary leaves and no process score or multiplier. Historical criteria/weights above are archival, not active scoring. Public inputs and author-route contract remain byte-identical. Existing acceptance remains PASS_STRICT_CURRENT_FROZEN_SCOPE; this migration is not new chemical validation. Fixed windows and retained boundaries are in scoring_rules.json and reference_qualification.json.


## 2026-10-19 Round3 scoring-contract repair

Historical verification statements above are preserved verbatim. Current mode-specific scientific rules supersede earlier scoring-policy wording; this append does not change the chemistry acceptance class.

- Audited every existing leaf for scientific role, source/verification support and physical definitions; retained unproven narrow-window risks as explicit provisional calibration limits rather than inventing wider tolerances.
